Title: Lettres édifiantes et curieuses, écrites des missions étrangères, nouvelle édition; Mémoires du Levant, Tome troisième
Creator: Jesuit missionaries of the Levant; the Armenian memoirs open with a letter of Father Monier, S.J., to Father Fleuriau; the editor of this edition is not named on the title page
Publisher: Paris: chez J. G. Mérigot le jeune, libraire, Quai des Augustins, 1780, avec approbation et privilège du Roi
Publication Date: 1780
Rights: Public Domain (published 1780, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item lettresdifiantes03jesu_0 (https://archive.org/details/lettresdifiantes03jesu_0), the item's own lettresdifiantes03jesu_0_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1780. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e378e1c590a9da2c8abc7f54413ac2667f8b50da2189688a966c6856956783bc.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. John Carter Brown Library scan; readable with the long s kept. The table at the end gives, for each piece, its place in the earlier edition (the Nouveaux mémoires des missions de la Compagnie de Jésus dans le Levant).
Content note: The Jesuits' memoirs on their Armenian missions: a history of Armenia and its patriarchs, the establishment of Christianity there, 'Du Rit des Arméniens schismatiques', 'Des erreurs des Arméniens', 'Manière de traiter avec les Arméniens', and the memoirs of the missions of Erivan and Erzurum; also letters on the Crimea, Circassia and Ethiopia.

Circafles  de  la  plaine,  &  les  Circafles
